{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"prefixes","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"præfix","tags":["alternative","obsolete"]},{"form":"prefixe","raw_tags":["for the verb"],"tags":["alternative","obsolete"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"antonyms":[{"word":"suffix"}],"glosses":["Something placed before another","A morpheme added to the beginning of a word to modify its meaning, for example as, pre- in prefix, con- in conjure, re- in reheat, etc."],"synonyms":[{"tags":["nonstandard","rare"],"word":"forefix"},{"tags":["archaic"],"word":"prefixum"}],"topics":["grammar","human-sciences","linguistic-morphology","linguistics","morphology","sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"in the UK, a number with an 0800 prefix is a toll-free number.","type":"example"},{"text":"Add the prefix +34 to dial a Spanish number from abroad","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Something placed before another","A set of digits placed before a telephone number, to indicate where the number is based, what type of phone number it is (landline, mobile, toll-free, premium rate etc.)"],"topics":["communications","electrical-engineering","engineering","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","telecommunications"]},{"glosses":["Something placed before another","A title added to a person's name, such as Mr. or Dr."]},{"examples":[{"text":"The string \"abra\" is both a prefix and a suffix of the string \"abracadabra\".","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Something placed before another","An initial segment of a string of characters."],"topics":["computing","engineering","mathematics","natural-sciences","physical-sciences","sciences"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈpɹiːfɪks/"},{"ipa":"/pɹɛˈfɪks/"}],"upstream_line":43182,"upstream_offset":297675249,"upstream_record_sha256":"0f3b70b74ac56c7877f4b2ab0e7d24013f83e5858e0d47d6d0c95fb0ee85059f","word":"prefix"}
{"etymology_number":"2","forms":[{"form":"prefixes","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"prefixing","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"prefixed","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"prefixed","tags":["past"]},{"form":"præfix","tags":["alternative","obsolete"]},{"form":"prefixe","raw_tags":["for the verb"],"tags":["alternative","obsolete"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"They duly arrived at the prefixed hour.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To determine beforehand; to set in advance."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"His requests for money were usually prefixed by an apology.","type":"example"},{"text":"You must prefix the number with the area code.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To put or fix before, or at the beginning of something; to place at the start."],"tags":["transitive"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈpɹiːfɪks/"},{"ipa":"/pɹiːˈfɪks/"},{"ipa":"/pɹɛˈfɪks/"}],"upstream_line":43183,"upstream_offset":297705577,"upstream_record_sha256":"eefe988844d3b9ffa75b2fca94009c7d03e515e53b2f55223289b901862d6d23","word":"prefix"}
